class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eats_scrapers\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003escrapers\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eGreenhouseScraper\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"anthropic\"\u003c/span\u003e).\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efetch\u003c/span\u003e()\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eScrapers are async-first — in async code (or for concurrency) use\n\u003ccode\u003eawait scraper.afetch()\u003c/code\u003e instead. The sync \u003ccode\u003efetch()\u003c/code\u003e also works from\ninside a running event loop (Jupyter, FastAPI): it transparently runs\non a worker thread.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eScraper adapters include:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMajor ATS platforms: ADP Workforce Now, Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, Workday, SmartRecruiters,\nSuccessFactors, Oracle, iCIMS, Workable, Personio, and more.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFirst-party company APIs: Amazon, Apple, Google, TikTok, and Uber.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePublic and regional sources: EURES, Bundesagentur, Arbetsformedlingen,\nWelcome to the Jungle, and others.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eRun \u003ccode\u003epython -c \"from ats_scrapers import list_ats; print(*list_ats())\"\u003c/code\u003e for the\nsources currently present in the hosted dataset. The dataset can list a source\nbefore this package ships a scraper for it — \u003ccode\u003esearch()\u003c/code\u003e still returns those\nrows; only building your own scraper needs one. To skip sources without a\nscraper:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" class="markdown-body entry-content container-lg" itemprop="text"><p align="center" dir="auto">
  <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kalil0321/ats-scrapers/main/assets/banner.jpeg"><img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kalil0321/ats-scrapers/main/assets/banner.jpeg" alt="ats-scrapers" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">ats-scrapers</h1><a id="user-content-ats-scrapers" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ats-scrapers" href="#ats-scrapers"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">An open dataset and Python toolkit for job data from ATS platforms and public
sources.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://pypi.org/project/ats-scrapers/" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/affc07fdbc7a90d27042e71b02f56fe33736d0bd331298ef1821e3ef907c02c1/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f707970692f762f6174732d7363726170657273" alt="PyPI version" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/ats-scrapers" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://pypi.org/project/ats-scrapers/" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/b0ba20d1a6e67f1fb1fada01343e47116868b29e230f849cc77ee7652292e6bd/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f707970692f707976657273696f6e732f6174732d7363726170657273" alt="Python versions" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/pypi/pyversions/ats-scrapers" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://github.com/kalil0321/ats-scrapers/blob/main/LICENSE"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/f8df3091bbe1149f398a5369b2c39e896766f9f6efba3477c63e9b4aa940ef14/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f6c6963656e73652d4d49542d677265656e" alt="License: MIT" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/license-MIT-green" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><code>ats-scrapers</code> provides two layers:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>A free, hosted dataset with <strong>4.2M+ live jobs</strong> from <strong>63,000+ companies</strong>
across <strong>49 sources</strong>.</li>
<li>More than 50 reusable scraper adapters, including Workday, Greenhouse, Lever,
Ashby, SmartRecruiters, and SuccessFactors.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Jobs are collected from ATS endpoints, company career sites, and public job
feeds, then normalized into one typed schema. Querying the hosted dataset
requires no API key or account.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Install</h2><a id="user-content-install" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Install" href="#install"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install ats-scrapers"><pre>pip install ats-scrapers</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The package is imported as <code>ats_scrapers</code>. Optional extras add only what you
need:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install &quot;ats-scrapers[parquet]&quot;   # query the full Parquet snapshot
pip install &quot;ats-scrapers[scrapers]&quot;  # run the scraper library
pip install &quot;ats-scrapers[all]&quot;       # install every runtime extra"><pre>pip install <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>ats-scrapers[parquet]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>   <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> query the full Parquet snapshot</span>
pip install <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>ats-scrapers[scrapers]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> run the scraper library</span>
pip install <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>ats-scrapers[all]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>       <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> install every runtime extra</span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Query the public dataset</h2><a id="user-content-query-the-public-dataset" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Query the public dataset" href="#query-the-public-dataset"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from ats_scrapers import search

# Per-source searches work with the base install.
jobs = search(
    query=&quot;machine learning engineer&quot;,
    location=&quot;Paris&quot;,
    ats=&quot;greenhouse&quot;,
    limit=100,
)

# The result is a pandas DataFrame.
print(jobs[[&quot;company&quot;, &quot;title&quot;, &quot;location&quot;, &quot;apply_url&quot;]])"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">ats_scrapers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">search</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Per-source searches work with the base install.</span>
<span class="pl-s1">jobs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">search</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">query</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"machine learning engineer"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">location</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"Paris"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">ats</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"greenhouse"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">limit</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">100</span>,
)

<span class="pl-c"># The result is a pandas DataFrame.</span>
<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">jobs</span>[[<span class="pl-s">"company"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"title"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"location"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"apply_url"</span>]])</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">For practical full-dataset queries, install the <code>parquet</code> extra. The base
install is intended for smaller per-source CSV slices.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from ats_scrapers import search

jobs = search(query=&quot;data engineer&quot;, remote=True, salary_min=80_000)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">ats_scrapers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">search</span>

<span class="pl-s1">jobs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">search</span>(<span class="pl-s1">query</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"data engineer"</span>, <span class="pl-s1">remote</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>, <span class="pl-s1">salary_min</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">80_000</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The <a href="https://storage.stapply.ai/jobhive/v1/manifest.json" rel="nofollow">live manifest</a>
contains current row counts and artifact URLs. See the
<a href="https://github.com/kalil0321/ats-scrapers/blob/main/docs/JOB_SCHEMA.md">job schema</a>
for field definitions and normalization rules.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Scrape a company</h2><a id="user-content-scrape-a-company" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Scrape a company" href="#scrape-a-company"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You don't need to know which ATS a company uses. Paste its careers URL:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from ats_scrapers import get_scraper_for_url

scraper = get_scraper_for_url(&quot;https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/openai&quot;)
jobs = scraper.fetch()"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">ats_scrapers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">get_scraper_for_url</span>

<span class="pl-s1">scraper</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">get_scraper_for_url</span>(<span class="pl-s">"https://jobs.ashbyhq.com/openai"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">jobs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">scraper</span>.<span class="pl-c1">fetch</span>()</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Or look it up by name in the hosted companies directory (63,000+ tenants):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from ats_scrapers import find_company
from ats_scrapers.scrapers import get_scraper

find_company(&quot;openai&quot;)          # → ats=&quot;ashby&quot;, slug=&quot;openai&quot;, url=...
scraper = get_scraper(&quot;ashby&quot;, &quot;openai&quot;)
jobs = scraper.fetch()"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">ats_scrapers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">find_company</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">ats_scrapers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">scrapers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">get_scraper</span>

<span class="pl-en">find_company</span>(<span class="pl-s">"openai"</span>)          <span class="pl-c"># → ats="ashby", slug="openai", url=...</span>
<span class="pl-s1">scraper</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">get_scraper</span>(<span class="pl-s">"ashby"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"openai"</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">jobs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">scraper</span>.<span class="pl-c1">fetch</span>()</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Scraper classes are also available directly:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from ats_scrapers.scrapers import GreenhouseScraper

jobs = GreenhouseScraper(&quot;anthropic&quot;).fetch()"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">ats_scrapers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">scrapers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">GreenhouseScraper</span>

<span class="pl-s1">jobs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">GreenhouseScraper</span>(<span class="pl-s">"anthropic"</span>).<span class="pl-c1">fetch</span>()</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Scrapers are async-first — in async code (or for concurrency) use
<code>await scraper.afetch()</code> instead. The sync <code>fetch()</code> also works from
inside a running event loop (Jupyter, FastAPI): it transparently runs
on a worker thread.</p>
<p dir="auto">Scraper adapters include:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Major ATS platforms: ADP Workforce Now, Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, Workday, SmartRecruiters,
SuccessFactors, Oracle, iCIMS, Workable, Personio, and more.</li>
<li>First-party company APIs: Amazon, Apple, Google, TikTok, and Uber.</li>
<li>Public and regional sources: EURES, Bundesagentur, Arbetsformedlingen,
Welcome to the Jungle, and others.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Run <code>python -c "from ats_scrapers import list_ats; print(*list_ats())"</code> for the
sources currently present in the hosted dataset. The dataset can list a source
before this package ships a scraper for it — <code>search()</code> still returns those
rows; only building your own scraper needs one. To skip sources without a
scraper:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from ats_scrapers import list_ats
from ats_scrapers.scrapers import ScraperRegistry, get_scraper

scrapeable = [a for a in list_ats() if ScraperRegistry.has_scraper(a)]"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">ats_scrapers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">list_ats</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">ats_scrapers</span>.<span class="pl-s1">scrapers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">ScraperRegistry</span>, <span class="pl-s1">get_scraper</span>

<span class="pl-s1">scrapeable</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> [<span class="pl-s1">a</span> <span class="pl-k">for</span> <span class="pl-s1">a</span> <span class="pl-c1">in</span> <span class="pl-en">list_ats</span>() <span class="pl-k">if</span> <span class="pl-v">ScraperRegistry</span>.<span class="pl-c1">has_scraper</span>(<span class="pl-s1">a</span>)]</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing</h2><a id="user-content-contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing" href="#contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Contributions can add a source, improve an existing scraper, or add companies
to the CSV inventories in
<a href="https://github.com/kalil0321/ats-scrapers/tree/main/ats-companies"><code>ats-companies/</code></a>.
The scraper API is intentionally tiny: subclass <code>BaseScraper</code>, set <code>ats</code>, and
implement <code>async def afetch()</code> using <code>self.make_fetcher()</code> for HTTP — retries,
backoff, and error mapping come for free. See
<code>src/ats_scrapers/scrapers/greenhouse.py</code> for a compact reference and the <code>Job</code>
model in <code>src/ats_scrapers/models.py</code> for the schema you populate.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/kalil0321/ats-scrapers
cd ats-scrapers
uv sync --extra dev
uv pip install -r pipeline/requirements.txt  # repo-only ops/publisher tests
uv run pytest
uv run ruff check ."><pre>git clone https://github.com/kalil0321/ats-scrapers
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> ats-scrapers
uv sync --extra dev
uv pip install -r pipeline/requirements.txt  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> repo-only ops/publisher tests</span>
uv run pytest
uv run ruff check <span class="pl-c1">.</span></pre></div>
